         .           /// stepping primitive — returning the destination as a raw bit for the
         .           /// u64-domain const builders. `try_offset`'s wrap test (Chebyshev distance
         .           /// ≤ 2) and doc 03 §3.2's (file distance ≤ 2 within 0..64) accept exactly
         .           /// the same steps for every step set used here: genuine knight/king/slider
         .           /// steps move at most 2 files and 2 ranks, while a horizontal wrap jumps at
         .           /// least 6 files.

         .               debug_assert!(0 <= sq && sq < 64);

         .           }

         .           /// Walk each direction from `sq` until the board edge or the first blocker;
         .           /// the blocker square is included in the attack set (capture semantics).
   538,880 ( 0.00%)  const fn sliding_attack(dirs: [i32; 4], sq: u8, occupied: u64) -> u64 {

         .               let mut d = 0;

         .                           break;

         .                   d += 1;
         .               }

          .           /// Parse an SKP1 section (starting at its magic). Strict on this build's
          .           /// [`BUCKETS`] and accumulator width [`crate::eval::nnue::sota_head::L1`];
          .           /// tolerant of further trailing bytes (future sections).
          .           /// Quantise the D47V output-skip lane to i8, one scale per bucket
          .           /// (perf study 8 §4.7).
          .           ///
          .           /// `w[b][i] ≈ q[b·L1 + i] · scale[b]`, with `scale[b] = max|w[b]| / 127` so each
-- line 512 ----------------------------------------
-- line 514 ----------------------------------------
          .           /// bucket-major and contiguous from [`parse_skip_section`], so a bucket is one
          .           /// slice.
          .           ///
          .           /// This is a lossy step and it is measured, not asserted: the study reports the
          .           /// cp deltas over the 800-FEN battery. It buys a ~6x cheaper lane — `vpdpbusd`
          .           /// takes 64 u8 x i8 pairs per instruction, where the f32 form needed a widen, a
          .           /// convert and an FMA per 16 lanes — for the one channel Stockfish gets free as
          .           /// the `FC_0_OUTPUTS + 1`-th row of the sparse matmul it already runs.
         10 ( 0.00%)  fn quantise_skip_lane(w: &[f32]) -> sota_head::SkipLane {
          .               let hl = w.len() / BUCKETS;
          .               let mut q = vec![0i8; w.len()].into_boxed_slice();
          .               let mut scale = vec![0.0f32; BUCKETS].into_boxed_slice();
          .               for b in 0..BUCKETS {
         24 ( 0.00%)          let row = &w[b * hl..(b + 1) * hl];
          .                   let peak = row.iter().fold(0.0f32, |m, v| m.max(v.abs()));
          .                   // An all-zero bucket keeps scale 0 and q 0 — it contributes nothing
          .                   // either way, and this avoids a 0/0.
         24 ( 0.00%)          let s = if peak > 0.0 { peak / 127.0 } else { 0.0 };
          8 ( 0.00%)          scale[b] = s;
         16 ( 0.00%)          if s > 0.0 {
      1,280 ( 0.00%)              for (dst, &v) in q[b * hl..(b + 1) * hl].iter_mut().zip(row) {
     52,480 ( 0.00%)                  *dst = (v / s).round().clamp(-127.0, 127.0) as i8;
          .                       }
          .                   }
          .               }
          4 ( 0.00%)      sota_head::SkipLane { q, scale }
          9 ( 0.00%)  }

          .               /// Draw by the 50-move rule or by repetition, relative to the search
          .               /// ply (doc 04 §3.9). Search scores a true result as `VALUE_DRAW`.
          .               pub fn is_draw(&self, ply: i32) -> bool {
          .                   let st = self.st();
          .                   // 50-move rule, guarded: a mating move ON the 100th halfmove still
          .                   // wins, so in check the claim stands only when an escape exists —
          .                   // checkmate outranks the clock (doc 04 §9.3, closed by
          .                   // design/05-movegen.md §3.9's `has_legal_moves`).

          .                   {
